# Household hh_s16 — 32 days, Monday to Thursday

Seed-generated household. Times are clock times; ids are the receptacle and object ids used in events.jsonl. A line indented under an activity says where an object went when the activity ended and why. WHIM marks a placement that landed somewhere other than where the decision was heading. Text in [brackets] names the hidden cause the decision is attributed to.

## Household

Type: flatmates. Rooms: balcony, bathroom, bedroom_1, bedroom_2, bedroom_3, entry, kitchen, living.

Residents:

## Day 4 — Friday

**Active causes**

- `ep:craving_a_snack:resident_1:d4`: Marco is craving a snack and fits one in when there is a gap; the bowl and glass tend to be left out (15:14–15:44).
- `running_late:resident_3`: Nora is running late all day (0.73): rushed putdowns, things dumped at the door, more whim.

## Day 7 — Monday

**Active causes**

- `ep:craving_a_snack:resident_2:d7`: Elena is craving a snack and fits one in when there is a gap; the bowl and glass tend to be left out (14:41–15:11).
- `ep:deadline:resident_2:d6:due_today`: Elena's deadline is at noon: a hurried morning, laptop and notebook never out of reach (06:30–12:00).
- `ep:deadline:resident_2:d6:relief`: Elena made the deadline and is running on empty: things get left out, and the evening is TV (12:00–24:00).
- `ep:early_night:resident_3:d7`: Nora turns in early tonight: a short evening, bed 45 minutes sooner (20:30–24:00).
- `ep:on_a_roll:resident_1:d7`: Marco is on a roll with work: sessions run long, breaks are short, the mug stays on the desk (13:03–16:09).
- `ep:rough_day_at_work:resident_3:d7`: Nora had a rough day at work: goes quiet in the evening, keeps the phone close, the jacket lands on the couch (17:30–24:00).

Internal states (0–1): Marco energy 0.55, hurriedness 0.23, distraction 0.39; Elena energy 0.34, hurriedness 0.42, distraction 0.36; Nora energy 0.49, hurriedness 0.52, distraction 0.32

## Day 9 — Wednesday

**Active causes**

- `ep:early_night:resident_2:d9`: Elena turns in early tonight: a short evening, bed 45 minutes sooner (20:30–24:00).
- `ep:restless_evening:resident_1:d9`: Marco can't settle this evening and wanders between rooms with things in hand (21:16–24:00).
- `ep:tidy_mood:resident_2:d9`: Elena is in a tidying mood for a while: a quick tidy of the living room, and things go back where they belong (12:14–14:14).
- `running_late:resident_3`: Nora is running late all day (0.78): rushed putdowns, things dumped at the door, more whim.

Internal states (0–1): Marco energy 0.36, hurriedness 0.47, distraction 0.09; Elena energy 0.50, hurriedness 0.47, distraction 0.41; Nora energy 0.48, hurriedness 0.78, distraction 0.66

- `low_energy:resident_2`: Elena has low energy (0.20): leaves things where they were used instead of putting them back.

## Day 16 — Wednesday

**Active causes**

- `ep:deadline:resident_1:d15:due_today`: Marco's deadline is at noon: a hurried morning, laptop and notebook never out of reach (06:30–12:00).
- `ep:deadline:resident_1:d15:relief`: Marco made the deadline and is running on empty: things get left out, and the evening is TV (12:00–24:00).
- `ep:sore_back:resident_2:d16`: Elena has a sore back today and sits in the armchair instead of the couch, so things land on the side table (06:30–24:00).
- `sick_day:resident_1` (Marco): Off sick, resting on the couch all day. no work or trips out; laptop, mug, medication, book and blanket all migrate to the couch and coffee table.

## Day 17 — Thursday

**Active causes**

- `ep:afternoon_slump:resident_1:d17`: Marco hits an afternoon slump: things get left where they were used for a couple of hours (14:41–16:40).
- `ep:deadline:resident_3:d17:looming`: Nora has a deadline tomorrow at noon: works into the evening, eats fast with the laptop at the table (18:00–24:00).
- `ep:early_night:resident_1:d17`: Marco turns in early tonight: a short evening, bed 45 minutes sooner (20:30–24:00).
- `ep:on_a_roll:resident_1:d17`: Marco is on a roll with work: sessions run long, breaks are short, the mug stays on the desk (13:31–17:28).
- `ep:on_a_roll:resident_3:d17`: Nora is on a roll with work: sessions run long, breaks are short, the mug stays on the desk (14:05–17:50).

## Day 18 — Friday

**Active causes**

- `ep:afternoon_slump:resident_1:d18`: Marco hits an afternoon slump: things get left where they were used for a couple of hours (14:33–16:05).
- `ep:afternoon_slump:resident_3:d18`: Nora hits an afternoon slump: things get left where they were used for a couple of hours (14:57–16:59).

## Day 26 — Saturday

**Active causes**

- `ep:afternoon_slump:resident_2:d26`: Elena hits an afternoon slump: things get left where they were used for a couple of hours (15:10–16:55).
- `ep:early_night:resident_3:d26`: Nora turns in early tonight: a short evening, bed 45 minutes sooner (20:30–24:00).
- `ep:on_a_roll:resident_2:d26`: Elena is on a roll with work: sessions run long, breaks are short, the mug stays on the desk (14:01–17:09).
- `ep:on_a_roll:resident_3:d26`: Nora is on a roll with work: sessions run long, breaks are short, the mug stays on the desk (13:28–16:57).
- `ep:restless_evening:resident_3:d26`: Nora can't settle this evening and wanders between rooms with things in hand (20:40–24:00).

Internal states (0–1): Marco energy 0.42, hurriedness 0.56, distraction 0.39; Elena energy 0.31, hurriedness 0.54, distraction 0.45; Nora energy 0.54, hurriedness 0.43, distraction 0.15

## Day 27 — Sunday

**Active causes**

- `ep:long_call_with_a_friend:resident_1:d27`: Marco gets a long call from a friend and takes it on the couch; the phone is left there afterwards (19:39–20:24).
- `ep:tidy_mood:resident_2:d27`: Elena is in a tidying mood for a while: a quick tidy of the living room, and things go back where they belong (11:44–13:44).
- `running_late:resident_1`: Marco is running late all day (0.91): rushed putdowns, things dumped at the door, more whim.

Internal states (0–1): Marco energy 0.54, hurriedness 0.91, distraction 0.31; Elena energy 0.48, hurriedness 0.41, distraction 0.50; Nora energy 0.38, hurriedness 0.45, distraction 0.36
